STATE BANK OF INDIA 
CENTRAL OFFICE 
Bombay, the 9th October 1975 

The following appointment on the Bank's staff is hereby 
notified 

Shri S. K. Shanna has been appointed as Deputy Branch 
Inspector on the Central OfiSce Staff as from the 
8th October 1975. 

The 17th October 1975 

The following appointment on the Bank's staff is hereby 
notified 

Shri S. 1. Vasudevan, has been appointed as Deputy 
Branch Inspector on the Central Office Staff as from 
the 15th October 1975. 

T. R. VARADACHARY, 
Managing Director. 

STATE BANK OF BIKANER AND JAIPUR 
Jaipur, the 11th November 1975 

Notice is hereby given that a fieneral Meeting of the 
Shareholders of the State Bank of Bikaner and Jaipur will 
be held at the Bank’s Head Office, Sawai Mansingh High- 
way, Jaipur on Saturday, the 3rd January 1976 at 11.00 A.M, 
(Standard Time) to elect two Directors under Section 25(l)(d) 
of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959 in 
niace of (1) Shri Lai Chand Kothari and (2) Shri Gopalkrishna 
Singhania who retire under the provisions of Section 26(2) 
of that act and who are eligible for re-election under Section 
26(3) of the said Act. 

By order of the Board 

SATYA DEV, 
Managing Director 


STATE BANK OF TRAVANCORE 
(Associate of the State Bank of India) 

HEAD OFFICE ; TRIVANDRUM 
NOTICE 

Trivandrum, the 7th November 1975 
With reference to the Notice of the 5th September, 1975, 
issued in terms of regulation 30(2) of the Subsidiary Banks 
General Regulations, regarding the holding of the General 
Meeting of the Shareholders of the State Bank of Travancore 
at the Head Office of the Bank, for the purpose of electirg 
two persons to be directors on the Board of the Bank, in 
pursuance of Section 25(1 )(d) of the State Bank of India 


(Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959, to fill up the vacancies which 
will arise on the 7th January. 1976, due to the retirement of 
(1) Shn C, P. Gopalan Nayar, I.A.S. (Retd.) and (2) Shri 
K. C. Kuruvilla, notice is hereby given that we have accepted 
the nominations woposing the names of Shri C. P, Gophlan 
Nayar, I.A.S. (Retd.), 763, Indira Nagar, H.A.L, Second 
.Stage, 100 Feet Road, Bangaiore-38 and Shri K. C. Kuruvilla, 
23-B/6 Nungambakkam High Road, Madras-34 who offered 
themselves for re-election in terms of Section 26(3) of the 
State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banka) Act, 1959. 

In the absence of any other nominations the General Meet- 
ing proposed to be held on Friday the 2ist November, 1975, 
stands cancelled in terms of Section 33(1) of the General 
Regulations and the above persons are deemed to be re- 
elected Directors of the Bank with effect from the 7th Januarv 
1976. 

S, NIYOGI, Managing Director

NATIONAL COOPERATIVE DEVELOPMENT COR- 
PORATION 

(Goieral Rieffulalions) 

New Delhi, the 1975 

No. NCDC.5-1/74-P&C. — In exercise of the powers con- 
ferred by sub-section (1), read with sub-section (2), of section 
23 of the National Cooperative Development Corporation 
Act, 1962 (26 of 1962), and in supersession of the National 
Cooperative Development Corporation General Regulalions, 
1966, the National Cooperative Development Coiporalion. 
with the previous sanction of the Central Government, licrc- 
by makes hte following regulations, namely ; 

CHAPTER I 
PRELTMINAry 

1. Short title and commencement ; (1) These regulations 
may be called the National Cooperative Development Corpo- 
ration General Regulations, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub'i- 
cation in the official Gazette, 

2. Definitions : 

In these regulations, unless the context otherwise 
requires : — 

(a) "Act" means the National Cooperative Develop- 
ment Corporation Act, 1962 (26 of 1962); 

(b) “Board” means the Board of MaBagemeiit of the 
Corporation constituted under Section 10 of the 
Act; 

(c) "Committee" means the Committee constituted by 
the Corportalon under section 11 of the Act; 

(d) "Chairman" means Chairman of the Board; 

(e) "Corporation” means the National Cooperative 
Development Corporation established under sub- 
section (1) of section 3 of the Act; 

(f) “General Council’ means the General Council of 
the Corporation constituted under sub-section (4) 
of section 3 of the Act; 

(g) "Managing Director" means the Managing Director 
of the Corporation; 

(h) "Member”means a Member of the General 
Council; 

(i) "President” means the President of the General 
Council; 

(j) "Secretary” jneans the Secretary of the Corporation; 

(k) "Vice-Chairman” means Vice-Chairman of the 
Board of Management; 

(l) “Vice-President” mefMis the Vice-President of the 
General Council. 

CHAPTER n 

3. Functions of the General Council ; The functions of the 
General Council shall be; 

(i) to lay down broad guidelines for achieving the 
obiectives of the Corporation; 

(ii) to ensure that the policy directives issued by the 
Central Government and priorities laid down by 
them are implemented; 

(iii) to review periodically the implementation of various 
programmes sponsored by the Corporation; 

(iv) to consider Programme of Activities of Ihc Cor- 
poration; and 

(v) to consider the Annual Report and Audited State- 
ment of Accounts of the Corporation. 

4. Fmfctions of the Board : The functions of the Board 

shall bo; ■ -- 

(a) to implement the guidelines laid down by the Gene- 
ral Council; 



part III— Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, NOVEMBER 22, 1975 (AGRAHAYANA 1, 1897) 


1995 


(b) to lay down subsidiary policies; 

(c) to prescribe criteria, conditions and patterns in res- 
pect of financial assistance for various programmes; 

(d) to sanction specific projects in respect of which the 
assistance of the Corporation exceeds Rs. 1 crore or 
as may be laid down by the Board; 

(e) to undertake review and supervise implementation 
of various programmes; 

(f) to deal with general matters relating to staff and 
general administration of the Corporation; 

(g) to deal with matters relating to borrowing of money 
in terms of section 12A(1) of the Act; 

(h) to determine the lines of investment of the funds of 
the Corporation; 

(i) to frame detailed regulations for the conduct of 
business of the Corporation; and 

(j) to deal with matters relating to delegation of 
powers. 

CHAPTER HI 

Meetings of the General Council and Board of Management 

5. Meetings of the General Council and Board of Manage- 
ment : The General Council shall ordinarily meet twice a 
year and the Board may meet as often as necessary. 

6. Notice of Meetings : Ordinarily a notice of 14 days shall 
be given to the members for a meeting of the General Coun- 
cil and a notice of 7 days for a meeting of the Board. An 
emergent meeting of the General Council or the Board ma 
bo called at a shorter notice. 

7. Manner of convening meeting and business to be tran- 
sacted thereat : A meeting of fhe General Council dr the 
Board shall bo convened by the Managing Director in con- 
sultation with the President or the Chairman, as the case may 
be. The notice of a meeting of the General Council or the 
Board shall bo sent to each member of the General Council 
or fhe_ Board, as the case may be, at his registered address 
specifying tho time, date and place of the meeting and shall 
also specify the business to be transacted at the meeting. No 
b'lsiness other than that specified in the notice or of which 
clear 7 davs notice has been given shall be transacted at a 
meeting. However, with the permission of the member pre- 
siding at the meeting, any other matter may be considered. 

R. Presiding Officer ; (11 Tho President or in his absence 

the Vice-President or in the absence of both, any member 
chosen bv the members present from amongst themselves, 
shall preside at a meeting of tho General Council, 

(21 The Chairman or in his absence the Vice-Chairman or 
in the absence of both, any member chosen by the mcmbcrc 
present from amongst themselves, shall preside at a meeting 
of the Board. 

9. Decision by Maioritv : All questions at a meeting of 
tho Genend Council or the Board, ns fhe case may be. shai' 
he decided by a majority of votes. In case of equalitv o*^ 
votes, the member presiding shall have and exercise a second 
casting vote. 

10. Ouornm ; 15 members shall form a quorum for a 

meeting of the General Council and 5 members shall form a 
Quorum of a meeting of the Board. If there is no quorums 
at anv mcofine of the General Council or the Board, as th" 
ease may ho. the meeting shall be adonrned. At ibe '■*d- 
i'viirned meeting, the business of the last meeting shall be 
conducted irrespective of whether there is qiionim or not, 

11. Place of Meeting : The meeting of the General Conn- 
ed and the Board shall be held at New Delhi or at an^ 
other place in India ns may bo decided bv the President of 
the General Council or the Chairman of the Board, as the 
easo may be. 

12. Record of minutes : A record of minutes shall h" 
maintained in which typed nroceedines of the meetings ol 
tpe General Council shall be kept. A similar record sh^ll 
b- maintained for the proceedings of the Board The pro- 
ceedings of a meeting of the General Council or the Board

CHAPTER rv 

MEETINGS OF COMMITTEES 

13. Meetings of Committees : Committees of the Corpora- 
tion may meet so often as necessary. Ordinarily, a notice 
of 7 days shall be given to the members for a meeting of 
mch Committees. 

14. Emergent Meetings : Emergent meetings of the Com- 
mittees may be called at a shorter notice, if so required by 
the Managing Director. 

15. Manner of convening meetings of Committees and 
business to be transacted thereat : Meetings of the Com- 
mittees shall be convened by the Managing Director or yn 
officer of the Corporation authorised by him and a notice 
of the same shall be sent to each member of the Committee 
at his registered address, specifying the time, date and place 
of the, meeting as well as the business to be transacted at the 
meeting. No business other than that specified in the notice 
or for which necessary prior notice has been given shall be 
transacted at a meeting. However, with the permission of 
the member of the Committee presiding at the meeting, any 
other matter may be considered. 

16. Presiding Officer : The Chairman of the Committee 
or in his absence, any member chosen by the members of 
the Committee present amongst themselves, shall preside at a 
meeting. 

17 Place of Meetings ; The meetings of the Committees 
may be held at New Delhi or at any other convenient place 
in India as may be decided by the Managing Director. 

18. Proceedings of the Meetings : The proceedings of 
the meetings of the Committees shall be circulated as soon 
as possible after the approval by the member presiding over 
such meetings for the information of the members of the 
C'ommittce and shall be placed before the next meeting.

to the members of the General Council or tho Board and

(a) Traveltlng Allowance : 

(i) Journey by Air : One standard fare plus l/5th 
thereof subject to a maximum of Rs. 20 for 
incidental expenses for each single journey. In

(either duo to jion-availability or to th© can- 
cellation of the connecting service), at an

lines Corporation does not provide at Its

to the touring member, half daily allowance at 
the rate applicable to that intermediate station 
may be drawn by him in respect of each such 
overnight halt in addition to the incidental ex- 
penses.

(iv) Journey by S/mmers : 1-3/5lh of fare at the 

lowest rate of first class fare.

day.

day; and/or

ing or on a later day. 

But he will be entitled to only half daily al- 
lowance for the day preceding and/or for the 
day following the meeting, if 

(a) he arrives a,t 12 noon or in the afternoon 
of the day preceding the day of the meeting, 
and/or 

fb) he departs in the forenoon of the day fol- 
lowing the day of the meeting. 

(c) Conveyance Allowance : 

A member, resident at a place where the meeling 
of the Committee is held, will not be entitled to

il of 1974), read with subrulc (lA) of Rule 6 of the Delhi 
Urban Art Commission (ferms and conditions of Service) 
Rules, 1974, the Delhi Urban Art Commission, with the pre- 
vious approval of the Central Government, hereby makes the 
following regulations regulating the method of recruitment to 
the executive posts in the Delhi Urban Art Commission, Now 
Delhi, namely ; — . 

1. Short Title : These regulations may be called the Delhi 
Uroan Art Commission (Executive posts) Recruitment Repti- 
lations, 1975. 

2. Application : These Regulations shall apply to the postt 
specified in column 1 of the schedule. 

3. Scale of pay: The scales of pay attached to the post 
shall be as specified in column 3 of the said schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications 

(i) The method of recruitment, age limit, qualificalions and 
other matters connected therewith shall be as specified in 
columns 4 to 8 of the schedule aforesaid, 

(ii) The selection for appointment to the posts shall be 
made by a selection committee comprising not less than one 
member of the Commission, the Secretaiy of the Commission 
and such experts as the Coromissjon may decide to co-opt. 

5. Disqualification : No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a 
person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or con- 
tracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for ap- 
pointment to any of the said posts. 

Provided that the Commission may, if satisfied that such 
marriage is permissible under the (xnsonal law applicable to 
such person and the other party to .the marriage and there are 
other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the ope- 
ration of this rule. 

6. Savliw ; Nothing in these regulations shall affect reser- 
vation and other concessions that may be provided for the 
Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and oither categories of 
persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time in this regard. 


No. 3(8)/74-DUAC. — In exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 27 of the Delhi Urban Art Commission Act, 1973
